Inheriting weights from pretrained Transformers provides an appealing shortcut, yet the fundamental representational gap between Softmax and linear attention prevents effective weight transfer. In this work, we address this conversion challenge from two perspectives: architectural alignment and representational alignment. We identify Test-Time Training (TTT) as a linear-complexity architecture whose two-layer dynamic formulation is structurally aligned with Softmax attention, enabling direct inheritance of pretrained attention weights. To further align representational properties, including key shift-invariance and locality, we introduce key instance normalization and a lightweight locality enhancement module. We validate our approach by linearizing Stable Diffusion 3.5 and introduce SD3.5-T$^5$ (Transformer To Test Time Training). With only 1 hour of fine-tuning on 4$\times$H20 GPUs, SD3.5-T$^5$ achieves comparable text-to-image quality to the fine-tuned Softmax model, while accelerating inference by 1.32$\times$ and 1.47$\times$ at 1K and 2K resolutions. Code is available at https://github.com/LeapLabTHU/Transformer-to-TTT.

</details>


### [148] [LIVEditor-14B: Lightning Unified Video Editing via In-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04569)
*Shitong Shao,Zikai Zhou,Haopeng Li,Yingwei Song,Wenliang Zhong,Lichen Bai,Zeke Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ISA（上下文稀疏注意力）框架，首次实现ICL视频编辑中近无损的注意力稀疏化，通过冗余上下文剪枝和动态查询分组，在LIVEditor-14B模型上实现约60%注意力模块延迟降低，同时超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频编辑向上下文学习（ICL）范式发展，但二次复杂度注意力机制造成严重的计算瓶颈，亟需高效的稀疏化方案。

Method: 1）高效预选择策略剪枝冗余上下文标记；2）动态查询分组机制，将高误差查询分配至全注意力，低误差查询分配至0阶泰勒稀疏注意力；3）构建1.7M高质量数据集及LIVEditor-14B闪电视频编辑模型。

Result: 在EditVerseBench、IVE-Bench、VIE-Bench上超越SOTA，注意力模块延迟降低约60%，实现近无损加速且不损失视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 所提ISA框架首次实现ICL视频编辑中近无损的注意力稀疏化，验证了上下文标记的弱显著性及查询锐度与近似误差的理论关联，为高效视频编辑提供新路径。

Abstract: Video editing has evolved toward In-Context Learning (ICL) paradigms, yet the resulting quadratic attention costs create a critical computational bottleneck. In this work, we propose In-context Sparse Attention (ISA), the first near-lossless empirical sparse framework tailored for ICL video editing. Our design is grounded in two key insights: first, context tokens exhibit significantly lower saliency than source tokens; second, we theoretically prove and empirically validate that Query sharpness correlates with approximation error. Motivated by these findings, ISA implements an efficient pre-selection strategy to prune redundant context, followed by a dynamic query grouping mechanism that routes high-error queries to full attention and low-error ones to a computationally efficient 0-th order Taylor sparse attention. Furthermore, we build \textbf{\texttt{LIVEditor-14B}} , a novel lightning video editing model via ISA and a proposed video-editing data pipeline that curated a 1.7M high-quality dataset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LIVEditor-14B achieves a $\sim$60% reduction in attention-module latency while surpassing state-of-the-art methods across EditVerseBench, IVE-Bench, and VIE-Bench, delivering near-lossless acceleration without compromising visual fidelity.

</details>


### [149] [Aes3D: Aesthetic Assessment in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05155)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Boyu Wei,Haoxian Zhou,Xuanhua Yin,Zihan Deng,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Aes3D，首个面向3D神经渲染场景的美学评估框架，包含Aesthetic3D数据集和Aes3DGSNet轻量级模型，可直接从3D高斯原语预测场景级美学分数，避免多视图渲染，在低计算开销下实现高效3D场景美学评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景评估方法主要关注重建保真度和感知真实感，忽略了构图、和谐度、视觉吸引力等高层次美学属性。同时缺乏带美学标注的3DGS数据集，且3DGS作为低层级原语表示难以捕捉高层级美学特征。

Method: (1) 构建Aesthetic3D数据集，提出针对3D场景美学的标注策略；(2) 设计Aes3DGSNet轻量级模型，通过美学监督学习多视角3DGS场景表示，直接对3D高斯原语进行操作，无需渲染多视图图像，直接回归场景级美学分数。

Result: 该方法在保持轻量级设计的同时实现了强性能，为3D场景美学评估建立了新基准。

Conclusion: Aes3D是首个系统性的3D神经渲染场景美学评估框架，有效解决了3DGS场景美学评估中缺乏数据集和高层特征提取难两大挑战，代码和数据集将在未来公开发布。

Abstract: As 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) gains attention in immersive media and digital content creation, assessing the aesthetics of 3D scenes becomes important in helping creators build more visually compelling 3D content. However, existing evaluation methods for 3D scenes primarily emphasize reconstruction fidelity and perceptual realism, largely overlooking higher-level aesthetic attributes such as composition, harmony, and visual appeal. This limitation comes from two key challenges: (1) the absence of general 3DGS datasets with aesthetic annotations, and (2) the intrinsic nature of 3DGS as a low-level primitive representation, which makes it difficult to capture high-level aesthetic features. To address these challenges, we propose Aes3D, the first systematic framework for assessing the aesthetics of 3D neural rendering scenes. Aes3D includes Aesthetic3D, the first dataset dedicated to 3D scene aesthetic assessment, built on our proposed annotation strategy for 3D scene aesthetics. In addition, we present Aes3DGSNet, a lightweight model that directly predicts scene-level aesthetic scores from 3DGS representations. Notably, our model operates solely on 3D Gaussian primitives, eliminating the need for rendering multi-view images and thus reducing computational cost and hardware requirements. Through aesthetics-supervised learning on multi-view 3DGS scene representations, Aes3DGSNet effectively captures high-level aesthetic cues and accurately regresses aesthetic scores. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves strong performance while maintaining a lightweight design, establishing a new benchmark for 3D scene aesthetic assessment. Code and datasets will be made available in a future version.

</details>


### [150] [InfoGeo: Information-Theoretic Object-Centric Learning for Cross-View Generalizable UAV Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07099)
*Hongyang Zhang,Maonnan Wang,Ziyao Wang,Hongrui Yin,Man On Pun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InfoGeo框架，基于信息瓶颈理论，通过最大化视角不变的目标中心结构关系对齐和最小化视角特定噪声信号，显著提升跨视角地理定位在多变环境下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角地理定位方法依赖全局特征对齐，难以应对区域纹理变化、天气条件差异等域偏移问题，尤其在无人机场景中，广阔视角引入密集细粒度目标造成严重视觉杂乱，亟需更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 受目标中心学习启发，将优化重构成信息瓶颈过程，包含两个核心目标：(i) 通过对齐跨视角的目标中心结构关系最大化视角不变信息；(ii) 通过跨视角知识约束最小化视角特定噪声信号。

Result: 在多种基准和挑战性场景下的广泛评估表明，InfoGeo显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: InfoGeo框架有效解决了跨视角地理定位中的域偏移和视觉杂乱问题，证明了信息瓶颈理论和目标中心学习在该任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Cross-view geo-localization (CVGL) is fundamental for precise localization and navigation in GPS-denied environments, aiming to match ground or UAV imagery with satellite views. Existing approaches often rely on global feature alignment, but they suffer from substantial domain shifts induced by varying regional textures and weather conditions. This issue becomes even more pronounced in UAV-based scenarios, where the broader perspective inevitably introduces dense, fine-grained objects, creating significant visual clutter. To address this, we draw inspiration from Object-Centric Learning (OCL) and propose InfoGeo, an information-theoretic framework designed to enhance robustness and generalization. InfoGeo reformulates the optimization as an information bottleneck process with two core objectives: (i) maximizing view-invariant information by aligning the object-centric structural relations across views, and (ii) minimizing view-specific noisy signals through cross-view knowledge constraints. Extensive evaluations across diverse benchmarks and challenging scenarios demonstrate that InfoGeo significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [151] [CaC: Advancing Video Reward Models via Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Concentrating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11723)
*Jiyuan Wang,Huan Ouyang,Jiuzhou Lin,Chunyu Lin,Dewen Fan,Boheng Zhang,Haonan Fan,Fei Zuo,Jia Sun,Huaiqing Wang,Honglie Wang,Yiyang Fan,Zhenlong Yuan,Zijun Li,Yongrui Heng,Guosheng Lin,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CaC模型，一种基于视觉语言模型的由粗到精异常奖励模型。通过全局时间扫描定位异常窗口→细粒度空间定位→时空链式推理的流程，配合大规模生成视频异常数据集和三阶段渐进训练（监督微调+双轮GRPO强化学习），引入时序和空间IoU奖励，在细粒度异常基准上提升25.7%准确率，生成视频异常减少11.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在细粒度异常检测中难以同时精准定位异常的时间窗口和空间位置，且缺乏可解释的推理过程，亟需一种能够进行结构化时空推理的异常奖励模型。

Method: 提出CaC模型：推理阶段采用全局时间扫描→局部空间定位→时空链式推理的三步流程；训练阶段构建首个大规模生成视频异常数据集（含逐帧边界框、时间窗口和细粒度归因标注），设计三阶段渐进训练（单帧/多帧监督微调→双轮GRPO强化学习），并引入时序和空间IoU奖励来监督中间定位过程。

Result: 细粒度异常基准上准确率提升25.7%；作为奖励信号使用时，生成视频异常减少11.7%同时提升整体视频质量。

Conclusion: CaC能够稳定聚焦于细微异常，通过结构化时空推理实现更可解释的异常检测，并有效指导生成模型减少异常输出。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Concentrate and Concentrate (CaC), a coarse-to-fine anomaly reward model based on Vision-Language Models. During inference, it first conducts a global temporal scan to anchor anomalous time windows, then performs fine-grained spatial grounding within the localized interval, and finally derives robust judgments via structured spatiotemporal Chain-of-Thought reasoning. To equip the model with these capabilities, we construct the first large-scale generated video anomaly dataset with per-frame bounding-box annotations, temporal anomaly windows, and fine-grained attribution labels. Building on this dataset, we design a three-stage progressive training paradigm. The model initially learns spatial and temporal anchoring through single- and multi-frame supervised fine-tuning, and then is optimized by a reinforcement learning strategy based on two-turn Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Beyond conventional accuracy rewards, we introduce Temporal and Spatial IoU rewards to supervise the intermediate localization process, effectively guiding the model toward more grounded and interpretable spatiotemporal reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CaC can stably concentrate on subtle anomalies, achieving a 25.7% accuracy improvement on fine-grained anomaly benchmarks and, when used as a reward signal, CaC reduces generated-video anomalies by 11.7% while improving overall video quality.

</details>


### [152] [Diagnosing and Correcting Concept Omission in Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14270)
*Kanghyun Baek,Jaihyun Lew,Chaehun Shin,Jungbeom Lee,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于遗漏信号干预（OSI）的方法，通过放大文本嵌入中的特征信号来缓解多模态扩散Transformer在文本到图像生成中的概念遗漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiTs）在文本到图像生成中常出现概念遗漏现象，即指定的对象或属性未在生成图像中出现，影响生成质量。

Method: 通过对文本token进行线性探针分析，发现文本嵌入中存在表征目标概念缺失的"遗漏信号"（omission signal），进而提出OSI方法，放大该信号以主动催化缺失概念的生成。

Result: 在FLUX.1-Dev和SD3.5-Medium模型上的实验表明，OSI即使在极端场景下也能显著缓解概念遗漏问题。

Conclusion: 放大文本嵌入中的遗漏信号是一种有效缓解文本到图像生成中概念遗漏的策略，提升了生成图像与文本描述的一致性。

Abstract: Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) have achieved remarkable progress in text-to-image generation, yet they frequently suffer from concept omission, where specified objects or attributes fail to emerge in the generated image. By performing linear probing on text tokens, we demonstrate that text embeddings can distinguish a characteristic `omission signal' representing the absence of target concepts. Leveraging this insight, we propose Omission Signal Intervention (OSI), which amplifies the omission signal to actively catalyze the generation of missing concepts. Comprehensive experiments on FLUX.1-Dev and SD3.5-Medium demonstrate that OSI significantly alleviates concept omission even in extreme scenarios.

</details>


### [153] [GHOST: Geometry-Hierarchical Online Streaming Token Eviction for Efficient 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15852)
*Leyang Chen,Junyi Wu,Zhiteng Li,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GHOST是一个训练无关的KV缓存管理框架，用于长单目视频序列的流式3D重建。它利用模型自身的3D几何输出，通过层级双重要得分机制、特权保护机制和余弦相似度引导的逐层预算分配，在线淘汰冗余token，在保持重建质量的同时将KV缓存减少近一半，推理速度提升1.75倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长单目视频序列的流式3D重建中，KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，造成严重内存瓶颈。现有方法要么截断缓存导致重建质量下降，要么依赖注意力分数启发式方法，无法保留几何上有价值的token。

Method: GHOST包含三项相互增强的创新：1）层级双重要得分机制，用于评估token重要性；2）特权机制，保护特殊token不被淘汰；3）余弦相似度引导的逐层预算分配，实现各层缓存预算的合理分配。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，GHOST在保持优秀重建质量的同时，将KV缓存减少近一半，推理速度比现有最先进方法快1.75倍。

Conclusion: GHOST利用模型自身3D几何输出来指导token淘汰，是一种训练无关、高效且有效的KV缓存管理方案，解决了长视频流式3D重建中的内存瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction from long monocular video sequences requires maintaining a key-value (KV) cache that grows linearly with sequence length, creating a severe memory bottleneck. Existing approaches either truncate the cache to a fixed set of anchor frames, leading to reconstruction quality degradation, or rely on attention-score heuristics that are agnostic to 3D scene structure, failing to preserve geometrically valuable tokens. To address these problems, we present GHOST (Geometry-Hierarchical Online Streaming Token Eviction), a training-free KV cache management framework that exploits the model's own 3D geometry outputs to evict redundant tokens online. GHOST introduces three mutually reinforcing innovations: a hierarchical dual-level importance scoring scheme, a privilege mechanism that protects special tokens from eviction, and a cosine-similarity-guided layer-wise budget allocation. Experiments on various benchmarks show that GHOST preserves excellent reconstruction quality while cutting the KV cache by nearly half and delivering 1.75x faster inference compared to state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/lokiniuniu/GHOST.

</details>


### [154] [Hilbert-Geo: Solving Solid Geometric Problems by Neural-Symbolic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16385)
*Ruoran Xu,Haoyu Cheng,Bin Dong,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Hilbert-Geo，首个面向立体几何的统一形式化语言框架，并基于该框架提出Parse2Reason方法（先解析后推理），在立体几何和平面几何推理任务上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何推理工作主要聚焦于平面几何，在立体几何上表现不佳，主要原因是3D空间图示和复杂推理的挑战。为填补这一空白，需要为立体几何建立统一的形式化语言框架。

Method: 提出Hilbert-Geo形式化语言框架（含谓词库和定理库），以及Parse2Reason方法：解析阶段利用条件描述语言（CDL）将自然文本和立体图像统一表示为形式化谓词；推理阶段基于CDL和定理库进行关系推理和代数计算，生成可验证的推理过程。

Result: 在SolidFGeo2k上达到77.3%（优于Gemini-2.5-pro的54.2%），在MathVerse-Solid上达到84.1%（优于GPT-5的62.9%），在平面几何数据集PlaneFGeo3k上达到80.2%的SOTA准确率。

Conclusion: Hilbert-Geo框架不仅解决了立体几何推理难题，还统一适用于平面几何，展现了良好的通用性。所构建的专家标注数据集和开源代码将推动几何推理研究发展。

Abstract: Geometric problem solving, as a typical multimodal reasoning problem, has attracted much attention and made great progress recently, however most of works focus on plane geometry while usually fail in solid geometry due to 3D spatial diagrams and complex reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce Hilbert-Geo, the first unified formal language framework for solid geometry, including an extensive predicate library and a dedicated theorem bank. Based on this framework, we propose a Parse2Reason method containing two steps of first parsing then reasoning. In the parsing step, we utilize conditional description language (CDL), a formalized language composed of predicates specifically designed to construct geometric conditions, to represent both problem description (natural text) and solid diagrams (visual image). In the reasoning step, we leverage those formal CDL and the theorem bank to perform relational inference and algebraic computation, generating strictly correct, verifiable, and human-readable reasoning processes. Notably, our proposed Hilbert-Geo is also applicable to plane geometry. To advance geometric reasoning, we curate two expert-annotated dataset SolidFGeo2k and PlaneFGeo3k, which are furnished with geometric formal language annotations, solutions and answers. Extensive experiments show that our proposed method achieves the state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance 77.3% in SolidFGeo2k and 84.1% in MathVerse-Solid (one small subset in MathVerse dedicated to solid geometry), substantially outperforming leading MLLMs, such as Gemini-2.5-pro (54.2% on SolidFGeo2k) and GPT-5 (62.9% on MathVerse-Solid). In addition, our method achieves the SOTA accuracy 80.2% in PlaneFGeo3k, demonstrating the generality of the Hilbert-Geo in geometric reasoning. Our code and datasets will be publicly available.

</details>


### [155] [Diffusion Models, Denoiser Architecture and Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16415)
*Itamar Levine,Yair Weiss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型的创造力来源，发现其源于去噪器架构与目标分布之间的交互作用，并通过理论和实验证明去噪器的归纳偏置必须与目标分布高度对齐才能生成成功样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能够生成与训练数据不同的高度逼真图像（即具有创造力），但已知若使用贝叶斯最优去噪器，模型只会复制训练样本。这一矛盾现象促使研究者探究创造力产生的真正原因。

Method: 理论方面，对三种去噪器架构（线性、多项式、瓶颈）给出了生成样本分布关于目标分布和去噪器架构的显式形式；实验方面，对流行的UNET去噪器架构进行微小改动，观察其对创造力表现的影响。

Result: 理论上推导出了不同架构下生成样本的显式分布形式；实验上发现UNET架构的微小改动会导致截然不同的创造力表现，且这些改动常产生高度非真实的样本。

Conclusion: 扩散模型只有在去噪器架构的归纳偏置与真实目标分布高度对齐时才能成功生成逼真且有创造力的样本。

Abstract: The creativity of diffusion models refers to their ability to generate highly realistic images that are different from their training data. Creativity is somewhat surprising since it is known that if the denoiser used in the diffusion model is the Bayes optimal denoiser for a given training set, then the model will simply copy the training samples. In this paper we present empirical and theoretical results that suggest that creativity in diffusion models is due to an interaction between the denoiser architecture and the target distribution. Theoretically, we give explicit forms for the distribution of generated samples as a function of the target distribution and the denoiser architecture for three different denoiser architectures (linear, polynomial, bottleneck). Empirically, we show that small changes in the popular UNET denoiser architecture leads to very different forms of creativity, and these small changes often yield samples that are highly nonrealistic. Taken together, our results show that diffusion models will only be successful if the inductive bias of the denoiser architecture is in strong alignment with the true target distribution.

</details>


### [156] [TWINGS: Thin Plate Splines Warp-aligned Initialization for Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22069)
*Hyeseong Kim,Geonhui Son,Deukhee Lee,Dosik Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TWINGS通过薄板样条插值（TPS）优化3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的初始化，解决稀疏视图下的点云稀疏性问题，在DTU、LLFF、Mip-NeRF360数据集上实现了更优的几何细节和颜色保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图输入下，现有的3DGS方法因点云初始化稀疏导致重建质量下降，难以同时保证几何结构细节和颜色保真度。

Method: 采用薄板样条（TPS）作为非刚体变形模型，通过最小化弯曲能量从控制点对应关系中估计全局一致的扭曲场，将反投影点与三角化3D控制点对齐，生成校准后的反投影点；并从这些点中采样靠近控制点的点，为3DGS提供快速且几何精确的初始化。

Result: 在DTU、LLFF、Mip-NeRF360数据集上的实验表明，TWINGS在稀疏视图场景下一致优于现有方法，重建结果具有更精细的结构细节和更高的颜色保真度。

Conclusion: 基于TPS的点云校准初始化策略能有效缓解稀疏视图下3DGS的点稀疏问题，是一种快速、几何精确且通用的改进方案。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse-view inputs poses a significant challenge in 3D computer vision, particularly for achieving high-quality scene reconstructions with limited viewpoints. We introduce TWINGS, a framework that enhances 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) by directly addressing point sparsity. We employ Thin Plate Splines (TPS), a smooth non-rigid deformation model that minimizes bending energy to estimate a globally coherent warp from control-point correspondences, to align backprojected points from estimated depth with triangulated 3D control points, yielding calibrated backprojected points. By sampling these calibrated points near the control points, TWINGS provides a fast and geometrically accurate initialization for 3DGS, ultimately improving structural detail preservation and color fidelity in reconstructed scenes. Extensive experiments on DTU, LLFF, and Mip-NeRF360 demonstrate that TWINGS consistently outperforms existing methods, delivering detailed and accurate reconstructions under sparse-view scenarios.

</details>


### [157] [JMed48k: A Multi-Profession Japanese Medical Licensing Benchmark for Vision-Language Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22080)
*Yue Xun,Junyu Liu,Qian Niu,Xinyi Wang,Zheng Yuan,Zirui Li,Zequn Zhang,Bowen Zhao,Shujun Wang,Irene Li,Kan Hatakeyama-Sato,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了JMed48k——一个日本多专业医疗执照视觉语言模型评估基准，包含48,862道试题和20,142张图片，覆盖11个国家考试（2005-2025年）。从语料库中提取了JMed48k-Eval子集（近5年12,484道题）。评估21个模型后，通过成对图像移除审计发现：闭源和开源模型从图像中获益显著，而医学专用模型对视觉证据的利用有限；闭源模型中图像移除效应在不同专业间差异可达7倍（从医师+5.7到公共卫生护士+39.8）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗VLM评估多聚焦于英文语境，缺乏多专业、大规模、带有结构化图像注释的日文医疗执照评估基准，无法支持可重复的专业分层评估。

Method: 从日本厚生劳动省官方PDF中构建了48,862道试题和20,142张图像，按8类视觉分类法标注；提取近5年12,484道题作为评估子集；设计成对图像移除审计方法，对比移除前后模型的答案状态四类转换。

Result: 评估21个模型（含闭源、开源、医学专用），分别报告纯文本和含图像性能；图像移除审计显示闭源/开源模型从图像获益大，医学专用模型对图像利用有限；闭源模型图像移除效应在专业间差异达7倍。

Conclusion: JMed48k为医疗VLM提供首个日语多专业大规模基准，揭示了不同模型家族对视觉信息的利用差异及专业间的显著异质性，支持可重复的专业分层评估。

Abstract: We introduce JMed48k, a multi-profession Japanese healthcare licensing benchmark for evaluating vision-language models. Built from official PDF materials released by the Japanese Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare, JMed48k contains 48,862 exam questions and 20,142 images from 11 national licensing examinations between 2005 and 2025, with visual content annotated under an 8-type taxonomy. From this corpus, we derive JMed48k-Eval, a recent five-year evaluation subset with 12,484 scored questions, including 9,905 text-only questions and 2,579 questions with images. We evaluate 21 proprietary, open-source, and medical-specific models, reporting text-only and with-image performance separately. Because these subsets contain different questions, we further introduce a paired image-removal audit that evaluates questions with images before and after removing visual content to explore four answer-transition states. The audit shows that proprietary and open source models gain substantially from images, whereas medical-specific systems show limited observable use of visual evidence, with many correct answers persisting after image removal. Even among proprietary models, the net image-removal effect varies sevenfold across professions, from +5.7 points on Physician questions to +39.8 points on Public Health Nurse questions. We release JMed48k to support reproducible, profession-stratified evaluation of vision-language models in medical licensing settings.

</details>


### [158] [Direct content-based retrieval from music scores images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22255)
*Noelia Luna-Barahona,Antonio Ríos-Vila,Félix Fuentes-Hurtado,David Rizo,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了音乐乐谱图像的内容检索方法，比较了基于光学音乐识别（OMR）的转录方法、无需转录的Transformer模型以及文本提示的大语言模型在四种不同语料库上的表现。结果表明，OMR方法在域内检索中表现更优，而无需转录的模型在处理域变化方面更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐乐谱数字化对保存和可访问性至关重要，但现有信息检索主要依赖标题或作曲家等元数据搜索，基于内容的乐谱图像搜索研究不足，但这对音乐家、音乐学家和教育工作者具有潜在价值。

Method: 首先研究乐谱中最相关的搜索特征，并定义从任意标注语料库构建查询数据集的系统方法。比较了三种方法：基于OMR的转录方法、无需转录的Transformer模型（直接从乐谱图像识别查询）以及文本提示的大语言模型。

Result: 在四种具有不同数据集规模、图像质量和排版机制的语料库上评估，发现每种方法在不同条件下各有优势：OMR流水线在域内检索中表现更好，而无需转录的模型在处理域变化方面更有效。

Conclusion: 不同方法各有所长，OMR方法适合域内场景，而无需转录的方法更具域适应性，为音乐乐谱图像的内容检索提供了多样化的技术路线。

Abstract: The digitization of musical scores plays a crucial role in their preservation and accessibility, yet information retrieval still depends mainly on metadata searches, such as by title or composer. Content based search in music score images remains underexplored compared to text documents, despite its potential value for musicians, musicologists, and educators. This work contributes to the field by first studying which characteristics of a score are most relevant for search and by defining a systematic method to build query datasets from any annotated corpus. We also consider diverse methods for content-based search on music score images, ranging from transcription-based approaches relying on Optical Music Recognition (OMR), to a transcription-free Transformer model trained to recognize queries directly from score images, and a text-prompted Large Language Model. Our experiments evaluate these models on four corpora exhibiting diverse characteristics in terms of dataset size, image quality, and typesetting mechanisms. Overall, each method excels under different conditions: OMR-based pipelines achieve higher in-domain retrieval, whereas transcription-free models handle domain variability more effectively.

</details>


### [159] [SCOPE: Simulating Cross-game Operations in Playable Environments for FPS World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23345)
*Zizhao Tong,Yeying Jin,Hongfeng Lai,Zeqing Wang,Zhaohu Xing,Kexu Cheng,Haoran Xu,Zhao Pu,Shangwen Zhu,Ruili Feng,Jian Zhao,Yan Zhang,Hao Tang,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE，通过将条件模块插入预训练视频扩散模型的每个Transformer块，实现像素级动作响应分离，并构建首个多游戏FPS数据集CrossFPS（69K片段，7款游戏），在密集控制信号下实现精准的局部动作效果生成与跨游戏零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPS交互式世界模型将动作全局注入并基于单一游戏训练，无法处理密集的帧级控制信号，且难以区分动作影响的局部区域与不受干扰的全局区域。

Method: 提出SCOPE，在预训练视频扩散模型的每个Transformer块中插入条件模块，将特征重塑为逐像素时间序列，使每个位置根据局部视觉内容计算动作响应，无需分割标签即可分离作用域内/外效果；同时构建CrossFPS多游戏数据集消除玩法偏差。

Result: 实验表明模型具有强动作响应性、精确的作用域分离能力和有效的跨游戏泛化能力，支持零样本迁移到未见场景。

Conclusion: 通过在预训练扩散模型中引入像素级动作条件分离机制，结合多游戏数据集训练，可显著提升FPS交互式世界模型在密集控制信号下的生成质量和跨游戏泛化性能。

Abstract: Interactive world models for first-person shooter (FPS) games must resolve high-frequency overlapping control signals at every frame without disrupting unaffected regions. Existing methods inject actions globally and train on single titles, failing under dense FPS inputs. We observe that FPS actions are spatially selective: discrete events such as firing or reloading affect only a localized region around the weapon (the scope), while continuous camera and movement signals govern stable surroundings. We propose SCOPE, which inserts a conditioning module into each transformer block of a pretrained video diffusion model. It reshapes features into per-pixel temporal sequences so that each position computes its action response from local visual content. This separates in-scope effects from out-of-scope generation without segmentation labels. We also introduce CrossFPS, the first multi-game FPS dataset with frame-aligned action telemetry. It comprises 69K clips from 7 titles with 10-DoF controller signals, curated to remove gameplay bias. The model learns general visual-to-action mappings rather than game-specific patterns, enabling zero-shot transfer to unseen scenes. Experiments confirm strong action responsiveness, precise scope separation, and effective cross-game generalization.

</details>


### [160] [PixIE: Prompted Pixel-Space Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23531)
*Ruirui Lin,Guoxi Huang,David Bull,Nantheera Anantrasirichai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixIE是一个基于视觉基础模型语义提示的像素级低光照图像增强框架，通过跨尺度去噪、DINO提示像素块（DPPBs）精炼、空间通道压缩（SCC）和多感受野像素嵌入（MRPE），在去噪与细节恢复上达到SOTA，PSNR提升1.9-15.0%，LPIPS降低8.5-44.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照图像存在严重噪声、对比度损失和语义模糊，使得增强成为一个去噪与细节恢复的联合问题，现有方法难以同时兼顾两者。

Method: 提出PixIE框架：1) 跨尺度去噪抑制噪声并保持结构；2) DINO提示像素块（DPPBs）注入中间DINOv3特征进行逐像素调制；3) 空间通道压缩（SCC）联合减少空间令牌网格和通道维度，实现高效像素级注意力；4) 多感受野像素嵌入（MRPE）提供邻域感知的像素表示，增强对信号相关噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在LLIE基准上，PixIE相比SOTA方法PSNR提升1.9-15.0%，LPIPS降低8.5-44.4%；定性比较显示更清晰的细节和更稳定的纹理，同时提升了重建保真度和感知质量。

Conclusion: PixIE通过语义提示的像素级前馈框架，有效解决了低光照图像增强中噪声抑制与细节恢复的联合问题，在定量和定性指标上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Low-light images suffer from severe noise, contrast loss, and semantic ambiguity, making enhancement a joint problem of denoising and detail recovery. We propose PixIE, a feed-forward pixel-space LLIE framework semantically prompted by a vision foundation model. PixIE first performs cross-scale denoising to suppress noise and preserve structure, then refines details using DINO-Prompted Pixel Blocks (DPPBs), which inject intermediate DINOv3 features through patch-conditioned, spatially continuous per-pixel modulation. To make pixel-space attention efficient across scales, we introduce Spatial-Channel Compaction (SCC), which jointly reduces the spatial token grid and channel dimension. We further propose Multi-Receptive-Field Pixel Embedding (MRPE) to provide neighborhood-aware pixel representations before semantic prompting, improving robustness to signal-dependent noise beyond point-wise embeddings. Experiments on LLIE benchmarks show that PixIE improves average PSNR by 1.9-15.0% over recent state-of-the-art methods and reduces LPIPS by 8.5-44.4%. Qualitative comparisons further show sharper details and more stable textures, improving both reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [161] [Nano World Models: A Minimalist Implementation of Future Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23993)
*Siqiao Huang,Partha Kaushik,Michael Chen,Hengkai Pan,Kaiwen Geng,Omar Chehab,Fernando Moreno-Pino,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Nano World Models 是一个轻量级、可复现、易扩展的代码库，基于扩散强迫（diffusion forcing）实现未来视频预测，提供统一接口用于研究世界模型中的生成目标、模型规模、动作条件化、潜在观测空间、数据集、评估协议和长程 rollout 等设计选择，并通过控制实验揭示了预测参数化、架构规模、动作注入、采样预算和领域复杂度对视频预测质量和自回归 rollout 行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管工业级交互式视频生成取得了快速进展，但研究社区仍缺乏紧凑、可复现且易于扩展的实现来系统研究现代世界模型的设计选择，现有实现常常将不同组件纠缠在一起，难以进行受控实验。

Method: 构建基于扩散强迫（diffusion forcing）的轻量级代码库 Nano World Models，提供统一的生成目标、模型规模、动作条件化、潜在观测空间、数据集、评估协议和长程 rollout 接口；在简单控制环境、游戏模拟和真实机器人数据上进行控制实验，分析预测参数化、架构规模、动作注入、采样预算和领域复杂度等因素的影响。

Result: 揭示了预测参数化、架构规模、动作注入方式、采样预算和领域复杂度等因素对视频预测质量和自回归 rollout 行为的具体影响；提供了开源代码、配置、评估脚本和预训练检查点。

Conclusion: Nano World Models 为开放、可复现、科学化的世界模型研究提供了一个紧凑且可扩展的实验平台，有助于社区系统性地理解世界模型的设计空间。

Abstract: World models have become a central paradigm for learning predictive simulators that support generation, planning, and decision-making. Yet, despite rapid progress in industry-scale interactive video generation, the broader research community still lacks compact, reproducible, and easily extensible implementations for studying the design choices underlying modern world models. We introduce Nano World Models, a minimalist codebase for future video prediction centered around diffusion forcing. Nano World Models provides a unified interface for generative objectives, model scales, action-conditioning mechanisms, latent observation spaces, datasets, evaluation protocols, and long-horizon rollout procedures. This design enables controlled studies of world-modeling components that are often entangled across separate implementations. Through experiments across simple control environments, game simulation, and real-robot data, we examine how prediction parameterization, architecture scale, action injection, sampling budget, and domain complexity affect video prediction quality and autoregressive rollout behavior. By releasing code, configurations, evaluation scripts, and pretrained checkpoints, Nano World Models aims to provide a compact yet extensible experimental substrate for open, reproducible, and scientific world-model research.

</details>


### [162] [Coarse-to-Fine Domain Incremental Learning with Attentive Distillation for Mining Footprint Segmentation in Multispectral Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24460)
*Alif Tri Handoyo,Vincent C. S. Lee,Rizka Widyarini Purwanto,Alex M. Lechner,Deanna Kemp,Muhamad Risqi U. Saputra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MineC2FNet框架，利用粗粒度边界数据通过粗到细的域增量学习提升细粒度采矿足迹分割效果，并发布了219张精标注图像的验证数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动且精细地全球采矿足迹分割对监测采矿的社会环境风险至关重要，但细粒度标注数据稀缺，而粗边界数据虽多却存在域偏移问题，难以直接用于提升细粒度分割。

Method: 采用教师-学生架构，在特征层和预测层进行注意力蒸馏，从粗域选择性迁移通用知识，同时利用有限的细粒度数据进行边界细化，实现粗到细的域增量学习。

Result: 与SOTA的域适应和域增量学习方法相比，MineC2FNet在有效处理域偏移的同时取得了更优的分割性能。

Conclusion: 粗到细的域增量学习框架能够有效利用丰富的粗标注数据提升细粒度采矿足迹分割，缓解细粒度标注稀缺问题。

Abstract: Automatically mapping and segmenting global mining footprints using remote sensing and deep learning is critical for monitoring the socio-environmental risks and impacts of mining, yet its progress is hindered by the scarcity of fine-grained annotated data. Although large-scale datasets with coarse boundaries are widely available, leveraging them to improve fine-grained segmentation is challenging due to significant domain shift. To address this, we propose MineC2FNet, a coarse-to-fine domain incremental learning framework that exploits abundant coarse data to enhance fine-grained mining footprint segmentation. MineC2FNet adopts a teacher-student architecture with attentive distillation at both the feature and prediction levels, selectively transferring generalized knowledge from the coarse domain while enabling boundary refinement using limited fine-grained data (fine domain). We further introduce an expertly validated dataset of 219 images with precise boundary annotations across diverse geographies and commodities. Extensive experiments against state-of-the-art approaches, including domain adaptation and domain incremental learning methods, demonstrate that MineC2FNet achieves superior performance while effectively handling domain shift. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/risqiutama/MineC2FNet.

</details>


### [163] [SRUG: Shadow-Guided Relightable Urban Scene with Generation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24700)
*Yonghao Zhao,Zexin Yin,Jian Yang,Beibei Wang,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRUG框架，利用阴影引导3D补全模型恢复不可见区域几何，结合迭代材质分解方案（基于大材质模型LMM）和物理光照模型，实现城市场景的可重光照，在视图合成和重光照任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市场景重光照存在两大挑战：1）场景无界且存在大量不可见区域，这些区域仍会投射阴影到可见区域；2）稀疏输入视图和复杂光照条件导致材质分解存在严重歧义。

Method: 1）阴影引导的3D补全模型（shadow-guided 3D completion model）恢复不可见区域几何；2）基于大材质模型（LMM）的迭代材质分解方案提供材质监督；3）物理光照模型（physically-based lighting model）捕捉复杂光照。

Result: 大量定量评估和视觉对比表明，SRUG在新视图合成和重光照任务上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SRUG通过阴影引导补全不可见区域几何、迭代材质分解和物理光照建模，有效解决了城市场景重光照的难题。

Abstract: Creating relightable urban scenes from images or videos is widely useful but highly ill-posed. Urban environments are typically unbounded and extend beyond the visible regions. As a result, many portions of the scene remain unobserved, yet these invisible regions can cast shadows onto visible areas. Reasonably modeling shadows cast by such invisible regions is challenging and poses a significant obstacle to creating relightable urban scenes. At the same time, sparse input views and complex illumination conditions further complicate relighting, as they introduce severe ambiguities in material decomposition. In this paper, we propose Shadow-guided Relightable Urban Scene with Generation model (SRUG), a novel framework designed to address relighting challenges in urban scenes. SRUG leverages shadows to guide a 3D completion model for recovering the geometry of invisible regions, promoting the synthesis of physically reasonable shadows. In addition, SRUG employs an iterative material decomposition scheme that applies the large material model (LMM) to provide material supervision and iteratively decompose the scene's material properties, enabling robust material decomposition. Building upon these components, we introduce a physically-based lighting model that captures the complex illumination of urban scenes and supports reliable relighting. Extensive quantitative evaluations and visual comparisons demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in both novel view synthesis and relighting tasks.

</details>


### [164] [VEOcc: Voxel-Centric Online Semantic Occupancy Prediction For Embodied Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25059)
*Ruoyu Wang,Yong Liu,Sheng Tao,Yuhang Lin,Yukai Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEOcc 提出了一种基于体素的在线3D占用预测与建图框架，采用递归感知-同化范式，无需初始尺度估计即可实现开放式地图扩展，并通过时空感知在线更新策略在离散体素空间中鲁棒聚合时序观测，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯的方法在结构边界保真度上存在困难，且严重依赖预定义的场景尺寸先验，限制了操作效率。为此，本文旨在消除初始尺度估计需求，实现高效的开放式地图扩展。

Method: 提出体素中心框架VEOcc，采用递归感知-同化范式。核心创新包括：跨时间逻辑聚合（TLA）保持时间一致性、可靠性感知置信度调节（RCM）校准空间不确定性、置信驱动增量状态更新（CSU）实现鲁棒全局状态同化。

Result: 在Occ-ScanNet和EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet上达到SOTA性能。零样本评估在自采视频序列上验证了其在完全未见过的真实环境中的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: VEOcc为自主探索提供了一种精确且高效的解决方案，无需初始场景先验，具备优秀的开放世界泛化能力。

Abstract: Crucial for autonomous exploration, online 3D occupancy prediction and mapping incrementally constructs dense spatial representations on the fly. However, recent Gaussian-centric methods struggle with structural boundary fidelity and rely heavily on predefined scene-size priors, fundamentally limiting their operational efficiency. In this work, we present VEOcc, a voxel-centric framework formulated as a recursive perception-and-assimilation paradigm. By eliminating the need for initial scale estimation, VEOcc enables highly streamlined, open-ended map expansion. Furthermore, to robustly aggregate noisy temporal observations within the discrete voxel space, we propose a Spatio-Temporal-Aware Online Update Strategy. It integrates Cross-Temporal Logit Aggregation (TLA) for temporal consistency, Reliability-Aware Confidence Modulation (RCM) for spatial uncertainty calibration, and Confidence-Driven Incremental State Update (CSU) for robust global state assimilation. % Extensive experiments on Occ-ScanNet and EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet demonstrate that VEOcc establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both local and embodied settings, providing an accurate and efficient solution for real-world exploration. Extensive experiments on Occ-ScanNet and EmbodiedOcc-ScanNet demonstrate that VEOcc establishes new state-of-the-art performance in both local and embodied settings. Notably, zero-shot evaluations on self-collected video sequences further confirm its robust out-of-distribution generalization capability in completely unseen real-world environments. Ultimately, our framework provides an accurate and highly efficient solution for autonomous exploration. Code and supplementary visualizations are available on our project page: https://wryzju.github.io/VEOcc/.

</details>


### [165] [Paris 2.0: A Decentralized Diffusion Model for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26064)
*Ali Rouzbayani,Bidhan Roy,Marcos Villagra,Zhiying Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paris 2.0是首个通过去中心化计算预训练的视频生成模型，相比单体模型，FVD从561.04降至279.01（提升约2倍），同时提高了CLIP文本-视频相似度和美学评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决去中心化训练下时序连贯视频生成的开放问题，证明视频生成也能像图像生成一样脱离单体GPU集群进行训练。

Method: 基于Paris 1.0（首个开源权重的去中心化扩散模型DDM）的配方，扩展至低分辨率文本到视频的去中心化训练。

Result: 在匹配总计算预算下，FVD从561.04降至279.01（~2.0x提升），CLIP文本-视频相似度和美学评分均优于单体模型。

Conclusion: 去中心化训练不仅能用于图像生成，也能有效用于视频生成，且性能优于同等计算预算下的单体训练。

Abstract: We present Paris 2.0, the first video generation model pre-trained through decentralized computation. Its training recipe builds upon Paris 1.0 (arXiv:2510.03434), the first ever open-weight Decentralized Diffusion Model (DDM), which showed that image generation can be trained without a monolithic GPU cluster. However, temporally coherent video generation had remained an open problem under decentralized training, and Paris 2.0 closes it.
  In low-resolution text-to-video training, against a monolithic model trained on the same data under a matched total compute budget, Paris 2.0 cuts Frechet Video Distance (FVD) from 561.04 to 279.01, a ~2.0x improvement, and lifts CLIP text-video similarity and aesthetic score.

</details>


### [166] [Reinforcing Few-step Generators via Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26108)
*Yushi Huang,Xiangxin Zhou,Ruoyu Wang,Chi Zhang,Jun Zhang,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RTDMD框架，通过两阶段训练（分布匹配蒸馏+奖励引导强化学习），在仅4步推理下实现少步文本到图像生成的最新最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步扩散蒸馏模型虽能高效生成图像，但难以与人类偏好对齐，现有方法在偏好、美学和组合性指标上仍有提升空间。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段提出AC-DMD，通过子区间分布匹配和一致性正则化辅助伪造评分模型跟踪生成器分布；第二阶段联合优化分布匹配项和奖励最大化项，其中奖励最大化项采用混合策略梯度（结合GRPO风格估计器与直接奖励反向传播），并引入SubGRPO降低方差。

Result: 在SD3、SD3.5和FLUX.2上，RTDMD在偏好、美学和组合性指标上均达到新最优，仅需4步推理，超越此前少步文本到图像方法。

Conclusion: RTDMD统一了分布匹配蒸馏与奖励引导强化学习，有效对齐少步生成器与人类偏好，建立新基准。

Abstract: Recent advances in few-step diffusion distillation have enabled efficient image generation, yet aligning these models with human preferences remains challenging. We propose Reward-Tilted Distribution Matching Distillation (RTDMD), a two-stage framework that unifies distribution matching distillation with reward-guided reinforcement learning for few-step flow generators. We show that minimizing the KL divergence to a reward-tilted teacher distribution naturally decomposes into a distribution matching term and a reward maximization term. In the first stage, we introduce Ambient-Consistent Distribution Matching Distillation (AC-DMD), which performs subinterval-wise distribution matching and augments the fake score objective with a consistency regularizer to help the fake score model track the shifting generator distribution under limited updates. In the second stage, we jointly optimize both terms: for the reward maximization term, we derive a hybrid policy gradient that combines a GRPO-style estimator for the stochastic intermediate transitions with direct reward backpropagation through the deterministic final step, and further introduce step-subset GRPO (SubGRPO) to reduce variance. Experiments on SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.2 demonstrate that RTDMD establishes new state-of-the-art results across preference, aesthetic, and compositional metrics with only 4 inference steps, outperforming previous few-step text-to-image generation methods. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Harahan/RTDMD.

</details>


### [167] [ChartAct: A Benchmark for Dynamic Chart Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26994)
*Muye Huang,Lin Wu,Lingling Zhang,Hang Yan,Zhiyuan Wang,Yumeng Fu,Zesheng Yang,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ChartAct，一个面向动态图表理解的交互式基准测试。现有基准主要关注静态图表，而真实世界图表常需要交互操作（悬停、点击、缩放等）。ChartAct收集了673个动态图表、1440个问答样本，评估了11个多模态模型和GUI智能体，发现最强模型Claude-Opus-4.7仅达到84.5%的成功率，多数模型低于60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表理解基准主要关注静态图表，但现实中的图表往往是动态和可交互的，关键信息需要通过悬停、点击、缩放、拖拽等操作才能获取。目前缺乏评估模型在真实交互环境中理解动态图表能力的基准。

Method: 构建ChartAct基准：从8个真实图表网站收集并筛选673个动态图表，覆盖7种常见图表类型，构建1440个高质量问答样本。每个样本在动态图表和仪表盘图表两种环境下实例化。基于此基准系统评估11个先进多模态模型和GUI智能体。

Result: 最强模型Claude-Opus-4.7达到84.5%的平均成功率，但大多数模型低于60%。进行了详细的失败归因和案例分析，揭示了现有模型在动态图表理解上的明显局限性。

Conclusion: ChartAct为研究真实交互环境中的图表理解提供了新的基准，揭示了现有模型在动态图表理解方面的不足，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Charts are widely used to present complex data for analysis and decision making. Existing chart understanding benchmarks mainly focus on static charts, but real-world charts are often dynamic and interactive. Key information may only appear after actions such as hovering, clicking, zooming, or dragging. Dynamic chart understanding therefore requires models to identify visible content, choose proper interactions, and reason over changing chart states. To evaluate this ability, we propose ChartAct, an interactive benchmark for dynamic chart understanding. ChartAct collects and filters 673 dynamic charts from 8 real chart websites, covers 7 common chart types, and constructs 1,440 high-quality question-answer samples. Each sample is instantiated in two environments, Dynamic Chart and Dashboard Chart, to evaluate dynamic chart understanding under different contexts. Based on ChartAct, we systematically evaluate 11 advanced multimodal models and GUI agents. Experimental results show that existing models still have clear limitations in dynamic chart understanding. The strongest model, Claude-Opus-4.7, achieves an average success rate of 84.5\%, while most models remain below 60\%. We also conduct detailed failure attribution and case analysis. ChartAct provides a new benchmark for studying chart understanding in real interactive environments. Codes at https://github.com/wulin-wulin/OSWorld_Chart

</details>


### [168] [Structure over Pixels: Learning Variable-Length Visual Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27696)
*Piotr Wyrwiński,Kacper Dobek,Krzysztof Krawiec*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STROP提出了一种离散视觉分词器架构，通过四阶段课程学习和基于DINOv3特征的率失真监督，能够在单次前向传播中自适应估计图像的程序长度，形成结构化场景表示，且代码长度随场景复杂度增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应分词器要么需要事后搜索，要么在预训练的离散码率中选择，无法学习与模型和场景耦合的连续每图序列长度；且通常针对像素重建训练，强调纹理而非结构。

Method: 提出STROP架构，包含一个专用的长度头；采用四阶段课程学习，通过冻结的DINOv3特征进行局部率-失真探测监督；绕过像素级重建梯度，完全基于高层潜在表示质量塑造码本。

Result: 程序长度随场景复杂度增长；在密集预测迁移任务和直接检查学习到的码本词汇中均观察到组合结构的迹象。

Conclusion: 通过结构化的场景表示和自适应长度学习，STROP能够生成与场景复杂性匹配的视觉程序，并展现出组合性结构特征。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokenizers translate images into ordered sequences of codes, providing a natural representation for structural description of scenes. Yet existing adaptive tokenizers either require post-hoc search or select among a discrete set of pre-trained rates, rather than learning a continuous per-image sequence length coupled to the model and scene, and they typically train against pixel reconstruction, emphasizing texture rather than structure. We propose STROP, a discrete visual tokenizer architecture that forms structural scene representations and simultaneously learns how long an image's visual program should be. Using a four-phase curriculum supervised by local rate--distortion probes against frozen DINOv3 features, STROP optimizes a dedicated length head that estimates the active prefix length in a single forward pass. By bypassing pixel-level reconstruction gradients, the codebook is shaped entirely by the quality of higher-level latent representations. Program length grows with scene complexity, and signs of compositional structure emerge both in downstream dense-prediction transfer and in direct inspection of the learned code vocabulary.

</details>


### [169] [ROVER: Routing Object-Centric Visual Evidence for Grounded Multi-Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27959)
*Guannan Lv,Ren Nie,Hongjian Dou,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ROVER是一种轻量级可学习插件，通过对象中心化差分注意力和步骤特定令牌三元组，实现高效全局视觉证据路由，在无需细粒度监督或复杂启发式规则的情况下，显著提升多模态大语言模型在多图像推理中的答案准确率和定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs的视觉证据利用方法存在两类局限：基于区域注入的方法削弱了整体场景理解和物体间关系，且解码成本随RoI数量规模增长；自适应视觉特征选择则需要细粒度监督或复杂启发式规则。两者均缺乏高效、可扩展的全局视觉证据路由机制。

Method: 提出ROVER作为轻量级可学习插件，在每个物体定位预测时，注入步骤特定的令牌三元组，协同实现：（i）聚合当前推理上下文；（ii）通过对象中心化差分注意力将图像内线索蒸馏到视觉工作空间；（iii）在该空间内跨对象和图像路由并整合历史感知证据。集成到Qwen2.5-VL-7B，采用交错SFT-to-GRPO训练流程。

Result: 在MM-GCoT上答案准确率提升4.8%，定位准确率提升14.6%；VideoEspresso上答案准确率提升8.6%。VideoEspresso训练模型展现出强迁移性，在多个基准上平均超越基座模型4.7%。

Conclusion: ROVER作为一种轻量级、可学习的全局视觉证据路由插件，有效克服了现有方法的局限，显著提升了多图像推理性能，且具有良好的迁移能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have increasingly localized and interleaved visual evidence for deliberative reasoning. Grounding-based approaches typically focus on regions of interest (RoIs) by injecting cropped image patches or RoI-specific features into the reasoning context. However, such designs can weaken holistic scene understanding and inter-object relations, while incurring decoding costs that scale with the number and size of RoIs. Alternatively, adaptive visual feature selection often requires fine-grained supervision or complex heuristics. To address these limitations, we propose ROVER (Routing Object-centric Visual Evidence for grounded multi-image Reasoning), a lightweight, learnable plugin for efficient global visual evidence routing. Upon each object grounding prediction, ROVER injects a step-specific token triplet to synergistically: (i) aggregate the ongoing reasoning context, (ii) distill intra-image cues into a visual working space via object-centric differential attention, and (iii) route and integrate history-aware evidence across objects and images within this space for subsequent reasoning. We integrate ROVER into Qwen2.5-VL-7B and develop an interleaved SFT-to-GRPO training pipeline. Strictly adhering to the original datasets and evaluation protocols, our method achieves the best performance on MM-GCoT (+4.8% answer accuracy, +14.6% grounding accuracy) and VideoEspresso (+8.6% answer accuracy). The VideoEspresso-trained model demonstrates strong transferability, outperforming the base model by +4.7% on average across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [CA-AC-MPC: CUDA-Accelerated Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29155)
*Antoonio Buo,Vittorio Cammarota,Michele Avagnale,Pierluigi Arpenti,Vincenzo Lippiello,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种CUDA加速的AC-MPC方法，通过加速可微MPC层的前向和反向传播，显著减少了训练和推理时间，同时在无人机竞速任务中保持了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AC-MPC虽然性能优异，但其可微MPC层需要反复求解优化问题，导致训练和推理延迟较大，成为实际部署的瓶颈。

Method: 引入CUDA加速技术来加速AC-MPC中可微MPC层的前向和反向传播计算，减少端到端执行时间。

Result: 在敏捷无人机竞速任务中，该方法实现了最先进的飞行时间和接近极限的动态行为，同时训练和推理时间显著减少。

Conclusion: CUDA加速能够在保证控制性能的前提下，有效降低AC-MPC的计算开销，提升其实用性。

Abstract: In the literature, actor-critic model predictive control (AC-MPC) integrates MPC with reinforcement learning to enable high-performance control of complex dynamical systems. However, its differentiable MPC layer requires repeatedly solving an optimization problem in both the forward and backward passes, leading to substantial training and inference latency. This paper tackles this bottleneck introducing a CUDA-accelerated variant that significantly reduces end-to-end execution time while preserving the control performance of the baseline formulation. Simulation results on an agile drone racing task show that our approach achieves state-of-the-art lap times and near-limit dynamic behaviour with markedly reduced training and inference time.

</details>


### [171] [Extreme dynamic symmetry enables omnidirectional and multifunctional robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29254)
*Jiaxun Liu,Boxi Xia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"动态对称性"概念，通过量化机器人质心加速度均匀性（动态各向同性），在1000+模拟形态和物理20足球状机器人Argus上证明：高动态对称性能持续提升轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、恢复力和能效，为设计敏捷、鲁棒、多功能的机器人提供通用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性在自然界中是核心组织原则，但在机器人设计中主要局限于几何形态，未能扩展到动态驱动能力层面。本文旨在将对称性从形态设计提升到动态驱动能力层面，探索其作为统一设计策略的潜力。

Method: 引入"动态对称性"概念，提出"动态各向同性"度量指标。通过在1000+模拟形态中进行系统研究，并开发Argus系列球形机器人（径向排列线性致动器直接塑造质心动力学），其中构建了物理20足Argus变体实现近极端动态各向同性。

Result: 高动态对称性一致提升轨迹跟踪、任务成功率、鲁棒性、恢复力和能效，动态各向同性越接近理论极限效果越显著。20足Argus实现了方向无关运动、杂乱可变形地形敏捷穿越、快速自稳和部分致动器失效下的恢复力；分布式感知支持全向感知和运动中的物体交互。

Conclusion: 不仅在设计形态上追求对称性，更在可达动力学层面追求对称性，为在不确定的陆地与地外环境中实现敏捷性、鲁棒性和多功能性提供了强大且通用的设计路径。

Abstract: Symmetry is a central organizing principle in natural systems, yet its use as a unifying design strategy in robotics has largely remained limited to geometric form. We show that symmetry can instead be leveraged at the level of dynamic actuation capability. We introduce dynamic symmetry, the uniformity of a robot's attainable center-of-mass accelerations, and formalize it through a measure coined as dynamic isotropy. Across more than 1000 simulated morphologies, we found that higher dynamic symmetry consistently improved trajectory tracking, task success, robustness, resiliency, and energy efficiency, with the benefits becoming most pronounced as dynamic isotropy approached its theoretical limit. To study this regime systematically, we developed Argus, a family of spherical robots designed to explore the effects of increasing dynamic symmetry. Members of the Argus family vary in their actuation geometry and dynamic symmetry level while sharing a common architectural principle: radially oriented linear actuators that directly shape the robot's center-of-mass dynamics. Among them, we built a physical 20-leg Argus variant that achieved near-extreme dynamic isotropy and demonstrated orientation-invariant locomotion, agile traversal of cluttered and deformable terrain, rapid self-stabilization, and resilience to partial actuator failures. Its distributed sensing further enabled omnidirectional perception and object interaction during continuous motion. These results show that designing robots for symmetry not only in morphology but also in their attainable dynamics provides a powerful and general pathway toward agility, robustness, and multifunctionality in uncertain terrestrial and extraterrestrial environments.

</details>


### [172] [MonoDuo: Using One Robot Arm to Learn Bimanual Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29298)
*Sandeep Bajamahal,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Toru Lin,Zehan Ma,Jitendra Malik,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MonoDuo框架利用单臂机器人演示结合人类协作，通过数据增强生成双臂机器人策略训练所需的合成数据，在五项任务中实现最高70%的零样本成功率，且通过25条目标机器人数据的少样本微调可进一步提升65-70%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂协调操作是真实世界任务的关键，但双臂机器人和数据集的稀缺限制了策略学习；而单臂机器人在实验室中广泛可用，因此提出利用单臂机器人数据训练双臂策略的方法。

Method: 通过遥操作单臂机器人完成双臂任务的一侧（人类协作完成另一侧），然后交换角色覆盖两侧数据；利用手部姿态估计、图像/点云分割和修复技术，将RGB-D观测增强为目标双臂机器人的合成演示数据，用于训练双臂策略。

Result: 在箱体搬运、背包整理、布料折叠、夹克拉链、盘子交接五项任务上，MonoDuo实现最高70%的零样本部署成功率；仅需25条目标机器人数据的少样本微调，相比从头训练提升65-70%成功率。

Conclusion: MonoDuo能够有效地将单臂机器人数据中的知识迁移到双臂机器人策略中，在无目标机器人数据时实现零样本部署，少量数据微调后性能大幅提升，展现了从单臂数据到双臂策略的高效知识迁移能力。

Abstract: Bimanual coordination is essential for many real-world manipulation tasks, yet learning bimanual robot policies is limited by the scarcity of bimanual robots and datasets. Single-arm robots, however, are widely available in research labs. Can we leverage them to train bimanual robot policies? We present MonoDuo, a framework for learning bimanual manipulation policies using single-arm robot demonstrations paired with human collaboration. MonoDuo collects data by teleoperating a single-arm robot to perform one side of a bimanual task while a human performs the other, then swapping roles to cover both sides. RGB-D observations from a wrist-mounted and fixed camera are augmented into synthetic demonstrations for target bimanual robots using state-of-the-art hand pose estimation, image and point cloud segmentation, and inpainting. These synthetic demonstrations, grounded in real robot kinematics, are used to train bimanual policies. We evaluate MonoDuo on five tasks: box lifting, backpack packing, cloth folding, jacket zipping, and plate handover. Compared to approaches relying solely on human bimanual videos, MonoDuo enables zero-shot deployment on unseen bimanual robot configurations, achieving success rates up to 70%. With only 25 target robot demonstrations, few-shot finetuning further boosts success rates by 65-70% over training from scratch, demonstrating MonoDuo's effectiveness in efficiently transferring knowledge from single-arm robot data to bimanual robot policies.

</details>


### [173] [The Open Motion Planning Library 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29301)
*Weihang Guo,Theodoros Tyrovouzis,Emiliano Flores,Clayton W. Ramsey,Zachary K. Kingston,Ioan A. Şucan,Mark Moll,Lydia E. Kavraki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMPL 2.0 是对开源运动规划库的重大升级，引入硬件加速以支持实时运动规划，并与现代AI研究工作流无缝集成，同时回顾了近二十年来该库与运动规划领域的共同发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有的OMPL库虽已成为运动规划领域的基石，但随着实时运动规划和AI研究的需求增长，亟需对库进行重大升级以满足新的应用场景。

Method: 通过硬件加速（如GPU/FPGA）提升规划效率，支持实时运动规划；与现代AI研究工作流（如PyTorch/TensorFlow等深度学习框架）集成，扩展原有架构。

Result: 发布了OMPL 2.0，实现了硬件加速和AI工作流集成两大新特性，同时延续了原有广泛的采样规划算法和状态空间支持。

Conclusion: OMPL 2.0标志着运动规划库从学术界工具向工业级实时应用的演进，展示了开源软件与学术领域共同发展的典范。

Abstract: The Open Motion Planning Library (OMPL), first released in 2008, has become a cornerstone of the motion planning community, providing implementations of a wide range of state-of-the-art sampling-based algorithms. Over almost two decades of continuous development, we have steadily expanded the library with new planners, state spaces, and problem formulations. These additions range from asymptotically optimal and lazy planners to constrained motion planning and planning with temporal-logic goals. Building on this foundation, we introduce OMPL 2.0, a major evolution of the library that targets real-time motion planning through hardware acceleration and integrates seamlessly with modern AI research workflows. We also reflect on how OMPL and the field of motion planning have grown together over the years, and discuss the library's broader impact on the research community.

</details>


### [174] [Phase-Conditioned Imitation Learning with Autonomous Failure Recovery for Robust Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29407)
*Dayuan Chen,Kai Tang,Yukuan Zhang,Kazuhiro Kosuge,Yasuhisa Hirata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于相位条件与力觉感知的鲁棒可变形物体操作框架，通过FiLM调节的ACT编码器、多模态相位预测器和混合阻抗控制器，在双臂挂取T恤任务上将成功率从56%提升至87%，并实现自主错误恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模仿学习策略（如ACT）在推理时依赖马尔可夫假设，当视觉上相似的观测需要矛盾的动作时会产生状态混叠，且无法从执行失败中自主恢复。为此，本文提出闭环分层架构以解决可变形物体操作中的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 1) FiLM调节的ACT编码器，根据当前任务相位调制特征提取，实现单一统一策略产生相位特定行为；2) 融合视觉、力和位姿反馈的多模态相位预测器，实时估计相位并检测视觉无法感知的接触失败，自主触发恢复轨迹；3) 混合阻抗控制器用于柔顺执行；4) 触觉遥操作接口用于力感知数据采集。

Result: 消融实验表明FiLM调制显著优于无条件和token级调节基线；t-SNE分析证实FiLM诱导出良好分离的相位特定特征表示。在双臂挂取T恤任务上，闭环系统通过自主错误恢复将成功率从56%提升至87%。

Conclusion: 本文证明了相位条件与力觉感知结合的分层闭环架构能有效提升可变形物体操作的鲁棒性，通过FiLM调制实现相位特异性行为的同时共享动作动力学，多模态相位预测器可检测视觉盲区的接触失败并触发自主恢复，为机器人操作中克服状态混叠和错误恢复问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a phase-conditioned, force-aware framework for robust deformable object manipulation. Standard imitation learning policies such as Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) rely on a Markovian assumption at inference, causing state aliasing when visually similar observations require contradictory actions and preventing autonomous recovery from execution failures. We address this with a closed-loop hierarchical architecture. A FiLM-conditioned ACT encoder modulates feature extraction based on the current task phase, enabling a single unified policy to produce phase-specific behaviors while sharing action dynamics across phases. A multi-modal phase predictor fusing visual, force, and pose feedback estimates the phase in real time, detecting contact failures that are invisible to vision alone and autonomously triggering recovery trajectories. The system is completed by a hybrid impedance controller for compliant execution and a haptic teleoperation interface for force-aware data collection. Ablation studies show that FiLM-based modulation significantly outperforms both unconditioned and token-level conditioned baselines, and t-SNE analysis confirms that FiLM induces well-separated, phase-specific feature representations. Validated on hanging and removing a T-shirt with dual arms, the closed-loop system improves the hanging success rate from 56\% to 87\% through autonomous error recovery. Code and videos: https://leledeyuan00.github.io/phaser/

</details>


### [175] [A Progress-Aware Leader-Follower Midair Docking System for Dual-Drone Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29410)
*Yifan Cai,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Xiangqi Li,Chenzhe Jin,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双无人机空中对接平台，采用主从编队和被动磁锁紧模块，实现小型四旋翼的可靠空中对接，并集成了完整软硬件栈以支持可重复实验和基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型无人机空中对接是实现模块化空中协作与操作的关键技术，但受推力、载荷限制，精确相对位姿控制与可重复性对接极具挑战，现有方案缺乏标准化评估平台。

Method: 采用主从编队控制、轻量化被动磁锁紧对接机构、进度感知任务管理器（分接近-对准-捕获-稳定四阶段），基于ROS 2集成Crazyflie/PX4接口，并实现同步日志记录。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，使用编队误差、基线/偏航一致性、对接成功率、对接时间、失效模式统计等指标进行了定量评估。

Conclusion: 该平台为空中对接的监督与同步策略提供了统计可靠的比较基准，为模块化空中协作和可重复空中操作建立了实用测试平台。

Abstract: Reliable midair docking between small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is essential for modular aerial cooperation and manipulation, but it requires precise relative-pose control and repeatable platform under tight thrust and payload constraints. We present a dual-drone docking platform where two quadrotors operate in a leader-follower formation and dock using a lightweight modular frame with passive magnetic latching. A progress-aware mission supervisor manages phase transitions: approach, alignment, capture, and settle. This platform integrates a complete hardware-software stack (ROS 2 with Crazyflie/PX4 interfaces) and synchronized logging for benchmark evaluation. We evaluate the platform in simulation and real-world experiments using quantitative metrics such as formation error, baseline and yaw consistency, docking success rate, time-to-dock, and failure-mode statistics. The platform enables statistically grounded comparison of docking supervision and synchronization strategies and provides a practical testbed for modular aerial cooperation and repeatable midair aerial manipulation.

</details>


### [176] [3DVLA: Enhancing Vision-Language-Action Models via 3D Spatial and Instance Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29416)
*Zhongyu Xia,Yousen Tang,Bingqing Wei,Yongtao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 3DVLA是一个即插即用的框架，通过多视角一致的3D特征编码、实例估计模块和掩码自监督3D编码分支，为预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型注入3D场景理解能力，显著提升机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏3D场景理解能力，表现为三个方面：缺乏多视角一致性的3D空间位置提取不足、3D实例理解不充分、遮挡下推理脆弱。直接集成成熟的3D感知方法存在架构不兼容和依赖昂贵实例级标注的问题。

Method: (1) 跨模态的显式多视角一致性约束与空间条件几何聚合的普适性3D特征编码；(2) 基于高层级实例令牌的实例估计模块实现3D实例感知；(3) 保留预测器进行视觉令牌补全的掩码自监督3D编码分支处理遮挡。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0数据集上，与多个VLA基线模型集成后，操作性能取得一致且显著的提升。

Conclusion: 3DVLA作为即插即用框架，能有效为预训练VLA注入3D推理能力，无需额外人工标注或丢弃VLM先验知识，兼具有效性与兼容性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models have achieved remarkable progress in robotic manipulation, yet they suffer from a critical limitation: a lack of 3D scene understanding. This deficiency manifests as three intertwined challenges: weak extraction of 3D spatial positions without enforcing multi-view consistency, inadequate 3D instance understanding, and fragile reasoning under occlusion. Although mature 3D perception methods exist, their direct integration into VLA pipelines is hindered by architectural incompatibility and by heavy reliance on costly instance-level annotations. To address the above challenges, we propose 3DVLA, a plug-and-play framework that injects robust 3D reasoning into pretrained VLAs without requiring extra manual labels or discarding VLM priors. Specifically, 3DVLA tackles the three challenges through: (1) pervasive 3D feature encoding with explicit multi-view consistency constraints across all modalities and a Spatially-Conditioned Geometry Aggregation method, (2) an instance estimation module with high-level instance tokens for 3D instance awareness, and (3) a masked self-supervised 3D encoding branch that retains its predictor for visual token completion to handle occlusions. We integrate 3DVLA with multiple VLA baselines and evaluate on LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin 2.0. Results show consistent and significant gains in manipulation performance, validating both the effectiveness and plug-and-play compatibility of our approach.

</details>


### [177] [ElegantVLA: Learning When to Think for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29438)
*Ye Li,Huanan Liu,Kangye Ji,Yuan Meng,Jiajun Fan,Yuansong Wang,Shiyu Qin,Chenglei Wu,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ElegantVLA 是一种即插即用的相位自适应推理框架，通过动态计算调度在视觉编码器、LLM和动作头之间分配计算资源，在不修改基础模型的情况下实现2.18-3.77倍加速，将控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型计算成本高、控制频率低，且现有加速方法对不同控制步骤采用固定计算策略，忽视了序列化具身控制中非均匀的推理需求。受人类运动控制中认知和反馈资源集中在目标敏感阶段的启发，作者提出VLA模型应学会何时投入完整计算、何时复用先前计算。

Method: 提出ElegantVLA插件式相位自适应推理框架，包含：(1) 轻量级调度器，通过时序表示相似性、机器人运动线索和回合进度联合分配计算；(2) 感知-语言推理：五级Vision-LLM计算模式（从完整重计算到多步时序复用）；(3) 动作生成：三级去噪模式（稳定运动时复用中间去噪状态，目标敏感阶段保留完整精炼）。

Result: 在GR00T上实现2.55倍加速，CogACT上实现3.77倍加速。在6个真实世界GR00T任务中，计算量减少2.18倍，控制频率从13.8Hz提升至26.3Hz。

Conclusion: ElegantVLA提供了一种通用的VLA加速框架，通过协调感知-语言和动作生成阶段的计算决策，在不修改或重新训练基础模型的前提下显著提升VLA模型的推理速度和实时控制频率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic control. However, their high computational cost and limited control frequency hinder real-time robotic manipulation, especially when large vision-language backbones and iterative action heads run at every control step. Existing VLA acceleration methods often optimize individual components or rely on fixed acceleration rules, treating different control steps with largely fixed computation and overlooking the non-uniform reasoning demands of sequential embodied control. Inspired by human motor control, where cognitive and feedback resources concentrate on goal-sensitive stages, we argue that VLA models should learn when to invest full computation and when to reuse prior computation. We propose ElegantVLA, a plug-in phase-adaptive inference framework that accelerates VLA models through intra-model dynamic compute scheduling. ElegantVLA introduces a lightweight scheduler that observes temporal representation similarity, robot-motion cues, and episode progress to jointly allocate computation across the vision encoder, LLM, and action head. For perception-language reasoning, the scheduler selects a five-level Vision-LLM compute mode, from full recomputation to multi-step temporal reuse, based on visual-language representation stability. For action generation, it selects a three-level denoising mode, reusing intermediate denoising states during stable motion while preserving full refinement for goal-sensitive stages. By coordinating these decisions, ElegantVLA offers a general acceleration framework for modern VLA pipelines with explicit action-generation modules, without modifying or retraining the base model. Experiments on GR00T and CogACT achieve up to 2.55x and 3.77x speedup, and on six real-world GR00T tasks ElegantVLA cuts computation by 2.18x while raising control frequency from 13.8 Hz to 26.3 Hz.

</details>


### [178] [VE2VF: Vision-Enabled to Vision-Free Distillation via Real-world Reinforcement Learning for Robust Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29564)
*Victor Kowalski,Chengxi Li,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种人在回路的强化学习框架，通过教师-学生蒸馏方法，将有视觉的教师策略知识转移到仅依赖位姿、扭转和力传感的无视觉学生策略中，在真实世界NIST装配基准板上，经过约50分钟训练后，在3个代表性任务上达到95%的成功率，并能鲁棒泛化到8个未见过的任务变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉驱动的强化学习策略容易过拟合训练时的视觉条件，限制其鲁棒性和可迁移性；而仅依赖本体感觉的策略训练速度较慢。现有领域随机化和数据增强方法在真实世界中难以应用。

Method: 提出人在回路的强化学习框架，采用教师-学生蒸馏范式：首先训练一个带视觉输入的教师策略，然后将教师知识蒸馏到一个仅依赖位姿（pose）、扭转（twist）和力/力矩传感（wrench sensing）的无视觉学生策略中。整个训练在真实世界中进行，无需领域随机化或数据增强。

Result: 在NIST装配基准板上，3个代表性任务经过约50分钟训练后达到95%整体成功率；能鲁棒泛化到8个未见过的任务变体；在最具挑战性的任务上通过蒸馏微调达到100%成功率；优于多种基线方法。

Conclusion: 教师-学生蒸馏方法能够将有视觉策略的快速训练优势与无视觉策略的强任务泛化能力相结合，在真实世界接触丰富的机器人操作任务中实现鲁棒且可迁移的策略学习。

Abstract: When using reinforcement learning (RL) for contact-rich robotic manipulation, vision can provide task-relevant information that accelerates learning beyond what proprioception alone can achieve. However, vision-enabled policies tend to overfit to the visual conditions seen during training, limiting their robustness and transferability. We present a human-in-the-loop RL framework that employs teacher-student distillation to achieve robust performance across multiple task variants, trained entirely in the real world without requiring domain randomization or data augmentation. A vision-enabled teacher distills its knowledge into a vision-free student that relies solely on pose, twist, and wrench sensing, combining fast training with strong task generalization. On the real-world NIST assembly benchmark board, our approach achieves 95\% overall success after approximately 50 minutes of training on 3 representative tasks, including robust generalization to 8 unseen task variants. Fine-tuning with distillation achieves full success on the most challenging task. We demonstrate that the resulting policies outperform baselines in both robustness and adaptability.

</details>


### [179] [Learning to Feel Materials from Multisensory Tactile Data via Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29572)
*Li Zou,Yasemin Vardar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的计算框架，利用多感官触觉数据建模人类材料感知与识别。该框架包含三个互连模型：从手指-表面交互特征映射心理物理感官属性、基于感知表征进行材料分类、直接从触觉特征分类材料。结果表明结合按压、静态接触和滑动交互可提高预测精度，热觉线索对感知建模和材料分类尤为重要，强调了热觉和柔顺性线索在机器人手指和触觉显示中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类对材料的触觉感知依赖复杂的多感官触觉线索，但低级触觉信号与感知表征之间的关系尚不明确，这阻碍了触觉在数字环境中的集成以及具备类人触觉感知的机器人开发。

Method: 提出了一个可解释的计算框架，包含三个模型：模型1将手指-表面交互特征映射到心理物理感官属性；模型2基于感知表征进行材料分类；模型3直接从触觉特征分类材料。采用按压、静态接触和滑动等多种交互方式，并融合热觉线索。

Result: 结合按压、静态接触和滑动交互能提高预测精度；热觉线索在感知建模和材料分类中信息量最大；热觉和柔顺性线索对人工系统近似人类材料感知至关重要。

Conclusion: 热觉和柔顺性线索在当前的机器人手指和触觉显示中代表性不足，融入这些线索可增强人工系统近似人类材料感知的能力，并为设计更具感知基础的触觉界面提供指导。

Abstract: Human tactile perception of materials relies on complex multisensory touch cues, yet the relationship between low-level tactile signals and perceptual representations remains poorly understood. This knowledge gap hinders the integration of touch in digital environments and the development of robots capable of human-like tactile perception. Here, we present an interpretable computational framework for modeling human material perception and recognition using multisensory touch data. Our framework comprises three interconnected models: Model 1 maps finger-surface interaction features to psychophysical sensory attributes, Model 2 classifies materials based on these perceptual representations, and Model 3 directly classifies materials from tactile features. The results showed that combining information from pressing, static contact, and sliding interactions improves prediction accuracy, and that thermal cues are particularly informative for both perceptual modeling and material classification. These findings highlight the importance of thermal and compliance cues, which remain underrepresented in current robotic fingers and haptic displays. Incorporating such cues may enhance artificial systems' ability to approximate human material perception and guide the design of more perceptually grounded haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [180] [How to Relieve Distribution Shifts in Semantic Segmentation for Off-Road Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29599)
*Ji-Hoon Hwang,Daeyoung Kim,Hyung-Suk Yoon,Dong-Wook Kim,Seung-Woo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ST-Seg提出通过风格扩展（SE）和纹理正则化（TR）增强语义分割模型在越野环境中的分布外泛化能力，有效应对源-目标域差异和传感器数据损坏导致的分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野环境中的语义分割面临源-目标域差异和传感器数据损坏等分布偏移问题，导致预测不准确和导航任务失败。现有方法隐式地在固定源分布内进行泛化，效果有限。

Method: 提出ST-Seg框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 风格扩展（SE），通过生成多样化真实风格来扩展源分布覆盖范围；2) 纹理正则化（TR），通过深度纹理流形稳定受风格增强学习影响的局部纹理表征。

Result: 在多种分布偏移的目标域上，ST-Seg显著优于现有方法，展现出强大的鲁棒性和实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: ST-Seg通过显式扩展源分布和稳定纹理表征，有效提升了越野导航中语义分割的分布外泛化能力，增强了其实用性。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation is crucial for autonomous navigation in off-road environments, enabling precise classification of surroundings to identify traversable regions. However, distinctive factors inherent to off-road conditions, such as source-target domain discrepancies and sensor corruption from rough terrain, can result in distribution shifts that alter the data differently from the trained conditions. This often leads to inaccurate semantic label predictions and subsequent failures in navigation tasks. To address this, we propose ST-Seg, a novel framework that expands the source distribution through style expansion (SE) and texture regularization (TR). Unlike prior methods that implicitly apply generalization within a fixed source distribution, ST-Seg offers an intuitive approach for distribution shift. Specifically, SE broadens domain coverage by generating diverse realistic styles, augmenting the limited style information of the source domain. TR stabilizes local texture representation affected by style-augmented learning through a deep texture manifold. Experiments across various distribution-shifted target domains demonstrate the effectiveness of ST-Seg, with substantial improvements over existing methods. These results highlight the robustness of ST-Seg, enhancing the real-world applicability of semantic segmentation for off-road navigation.

</details>


### [181] [VLAConf: Calibrated Task-Success Confidence for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29605)
*Dehao Huang,Aoxiang Gu,Chengjie Zhang,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Zilang Cen,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLAConf，一种基于单类判别式置信度框架，通过冻结的VLA内部表示和轻量级置信度头直接估计逐步骤异常分数，无需重复采样，在LIBERO基准和真实机器人实验中显著提升了置信度信号质量和推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型置信度估计方法（如集成方法、动作token概率）存在计算效率低和跨架构泛化性差的问题，且仅适用于离散动作输出，难以推广到连续动作空间。

Method: 提出VLAConf框架，利用冻结的预训练VLA内部表示，通过轻量级置信度头在单次前向传播中直接估计逐步骤异常分数；同时采用步骤条件建模来编码操作轨迹中的 rollout 阶段信息。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，VLAConf显著提升了事后校准中置信度信号的质量，推理效率大幅优于现有基线方法；真实机器人实验进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: VLAConf是一种高效且泛化性强的VLA模型置信度估计方法，适用于连续动作空间，为开放世界机器人操作任务的风险敏感决策和故障预测提供了可靠信号。

Abstract: Confidence estimation for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models is essential for robots to perform manipulation tasks in the open world, providing crucial signals for risk-sensitive decision-making and failure anticipation. Existing confidence estimation methods typically rely on ensemble-based paradigms or action-token probabilities to predict the likelihood of task success. However, they still encounter challenges in computational efficiency and cross-architecture generalizability. These methods usually require repeated sampling, leading to inference inefficiency, and are restricted to VLA models with discrete action outputs, making them difficult to apply to continuous action spaces. To address this issue, we propose VLAConf, a one-class discriminative confidence framework. By leveraging frozen pretrained VLA internal representations, VLAConf directly estimates step-wise anomaly scores in a single forward pass using a lightweight confidence head, thereby eliminating the overhead of exhaustive resampling. We additionally use step-conditioned modeling to encode rollout-phase information along the manipulation trajectory. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark demonstrate that VLAConf significantly improves the quality of the confidence signal constructed for post-hoc calibration, outperforming existing baselines by a large margin in inference efficiency. The effectiveness of VLAConf is further validated in real-robot experiments. To access the source code and supplementary videos, visit https://sites.google.com/view/vlaconf.

</details>


### [182] [PhAIL: A Real-Robot VLA Benchmark and Distributional Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29710)
*Sergey Arkhangelskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PhAIL物理AI排行榜，使用时间-成功累积分布函数（CDF）作为评估基元，引入人类相对吞吐量（HRT）指标和Kolmogorov-Smirnov显著性检验，在Franka FR3机器人上对四种VLA策略进行评测，发现基于二值阈值的指标在N≤30时无法分辨的对比，KS检验能有效区分，最佳VLA比人类参考慢约7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略评估依赖小样本（N≤25）的二值成功率，缺乏置信区间和统计比较，难以可靠地分辨性能接近的策略。

Method: 提出PhAIL基准测试平台，采用时间-成功累积分布函数（CDF）作为评估基元；引入人类相对吞吐量（HRT）标量指标配合bootstrap置信区间；使用Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验进行显著性比较，按物体宏平均。

Result: 在四种公开VLA策略上，宏平均KS检验在N≤30时成功区分了GR00T vs. ACT和OpenPI vs. ACT两组对比，而二值阈值指标无法分辨；OpenPI vs. GR00T仍无法区分；最佳VLA操作速度比人类参考慢约7倍（RMST比值）。

Conclusion: 分布化评估方法（CDF+HRT+KS检验）比传统二值成功率在小样本下更具统计分辨力，建议作为机器人策略评估的标准实践。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation of vision-language-action (VLA) policies still rests on binary success rate at a fixed timeout with $N \le 25$ rollouts per condition, almost always without confidence intervals or paired statistical comparison; these cohort sizes struggle to resolve close comparisons reliably. We introduce PhAIL (Physical AI Leaderboard, https://phail.ai), an open real-robot benchmark on a Franka FR3 (dataset, per-rollout artifacts, and end-to-end reference implementation) of a distributional evaluation methodology: the time-to-success cumulative distribution function (CDF) as the evaluation primitive, with two separated jobs. The first is scoring via Human-Relative Throughput (HRT), a dimensionless scalar with bootstrap confidence intervals, anchored to same-fixture human teleoperation. The second is a significance test (Kolmogorov-Smirnov, computed per-object and macro-averaged across objects). On four publicly-available VLAs, the macro-averaged KS test resolves two close comparisons (GR00T vs. ACT, OpenPI vs. ACT) at $N \le 30$ rollouts per (model, object) cell where binary-threshold metrics do not; the closest pair (OpenPI vs. GR00T) remains unresolved within our budget. The best evaluated VLA is $\sim 7\times$ slower per operation (RMST ratio) than the human reference.

</details>


### [183] [MARS Policy: Multimodality Only When It Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29766)
*Jindou Jia,Tuo An,Yuxuan Hu,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Bohan Hou,Xiangyu Chen,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MARS策略通过仅在需要多模态行为时注入随机噪声，在单模态阶段使用确定性学习，实现了生成式策略的多模态能力与确定性模型的高效训练推理之间的平衡，在仿真和真实任务中分别提升了成功率和降低了推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式策略通过随机噪声初始化和迭代去噪实现多模态行为，但存在训练复杂度高、推理效率低的问题；而并非所有任务阶段都需要行为多样性，因此提出了自适应选择性激活多模态生成的方法。

Method: 提出Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS)策略，自适应地在需要行为多样性的阶段激活随机噪声生成多模态行为，在单模态阶段退化为高效的确定性学习，仅在正确的时间注入适量的噪声。

Result: 在8个仿真和4个真实世界任务上验证，真实测试中成功率提升16.67%，推理延迟降低83.20%。在近确定性任务上，MARS反而比确定性策略训练效率更高，能更有效地建模细微的动作多样性。

Conclusion: MARS策略有效弥合了生成式策略的多模态能力与确定性模型的高效性之间的差距，证明选择性激活多模态生成是兼顾表达效率的有效范式。

Abstract: Imitation learning has become a cornerstone for solving complex robotic manipulation tasks. In particular, multimodality, which enables robots to capture diverse yet valid behavioral patterns, has driven the rapid emergence of generative policies as a dominant paradigm in robot learning. However, achieving such multimodality typically relies on stochastic noise initialization and iterative denoising procedures, resulting in substantial training complexity and low inference efficiency. Meanwhile, not all phases of a robotic task inherently require behavioral diversity. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Modality-Adaptive Robot Sampling (MARS) policy, which adaptively invokes tailored stochasticity only when it is truly beneficial, while reverting to an efficient deterministic learning during single-modal phases. In other words, the proper amount of noise is injected only at the proper time. By selectively activating multimodal generation, MARS policy bridges the gap between the multimodal capability of generative policies and the superior training and inference efficiency of deterministic models. Empirical studies across 8 simulated and 4 real-world tasks demonstrate that MARS exhibits robust multimodal expressivity and high efficiency, with a 16.67% success rate improvement and an 83.20% inference latency reduction in real-world tests. Counterintuitively, MARS also outpaces deterministic policies in training efficiency on near-deterministic tasks by more effectively modeling nuanced action diversity.

</details>


### [184] [Joint Angle Estimation with Customized Wristband Based on Online Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29771)
*Shuo Wang,Xiaobin Chen,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于在线增量学习的智能腕带系统，用于手腕关节角度估计。系统采用两阶段方法：第一阶段利用IMU数据通过在线学习更新模型，第二阶段仅凭腕带传感器进行角度估计。该方法能适应不同测试配置（左右手腕、佩戴位置偏移、不同受试者）带来的数据漂移，在不同场景下估计误差约为15度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴传感器研究应用较为简单，难以适应不同情境变化（如左右手腕差异、佩戴位置偏差、不同受试者差异等），缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 提出一种基于在线增量学习的两阶段腕关节角度估计方法：第一阶段，利用IMU提供的实时数据作为地面真值，通过在线学习根据佩戴者的手腕运动特征更新模型；第二阶段，仅使用腕带传感器进行角度估计。模型训练在数据采集过程中完成。

Result: 传感器在不同应变条件下表现出良好性能，该系统在多种场景下的腕关节轨迹估计误差约为15度。

Conclusion: 该在线增量学习方法能有效适应不同测试配置导致的数据漂移，包括同一受试者的左右手腕差异、同一手腕佩戴位置偏差以及不同受试者间的差异，提高了软体可穿戴传感器在运动监测中的适应性和实用性。

Abstract: Intelligent wearable technology plays an increasingly important role in human-computer interaction, motion, and health monitoring. To ensure comfort and practicality of use, one common form for motion monitoring is to utilize soft wearable sensors. However, many research applications regarding wearable sensors are simplistic and difficult to adapt to different situations. This study proposes a system for estimating the angle of the wrist joint using a customized wristband based on an online incremental learning approach. It is a two-stage estimation method: the first stage updates the model based on the wearer's wrist movement characteristics using online learning, integrating real-time data from an IMU as ground truth. The second stage utilizes the updated model for estimation of wrist joint angle solely with the wristband. In other words, model training is completed during data acquisition, allowing the trained model to be used for subsequent angle estimation. This method offers advantages in adapting to data drift caused by variations in different testing configurations, such as the left and right wrists of the same subject, deviations in the wearing position on the same wrist, and even differences among various subjects. The results indicate that the sensors exhibit good performance under strain variations, and the wrist joint trajectory estimation of the proposed system has an approximate error of 15 degree in different scenarios.

</details>


### [185] [LLM-Guided Future Hypotheses for Horizon-Aware Exploration in Multi-Step Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29864)
*Mohammad Khoshnazar,Andrew Melnik,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO
